## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```

```

```

  [partner message] Hi, I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can fix a failing test in one of your repos while you do something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 코드 수정, 웹 확인, 메일·일정 정리 같은 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 저장소에서 실패하는 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀해 주시면 바로 시작할게요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPとUDPの一番の違いは、**TCPは届いたかを確認して順番どおりに確実に届ける方式、UDPは確認なしで速さを優先する方式**という点です。

Webや メール のように正確さが大事な通信はTCP、動画配信やオンラインゲームのようにリアルタイム性が大事な通信はUDPが使われます。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para limpiar tu rama local antes de compartirla, es decir, para ordenar tus commits sobre la rama principal. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es pública o la comparten varias personas, porque reescribir su historia con rebase rompe el trabajo de los demás.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 是可靠的、面向连接的传输，保证数据按序、完整送达；UDP 不保证，只管尽快发出去，换来的是更低的延迟和开销。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to tidy its commits before a PR, so history reads as one straight line.
Merge when the branch is already shared or others have built on it, since rebasing rewrites history they depend on.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 用文件路径直接跟在 `bun test` 后面就行:

```bash
bun test tests/user.test.ts
```

只写文件名的一部分也可以,Bun 会匹配路径里包含这段字符串的测试文件:

```bash
bun test user.test
```

如果还想只跑文件里的某一个测试,加 `-t`(或 `--test-name-pattern`),后面写测试名的一部分:

```bash
bun test tests/user.test.ts -t "登录"
```
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log records every change to a database before that change is applied to the main data files. After a crash, the database replays the log to recover committed changes and roll back incomplete ones, so no data is lost or left half-written.
```

Place: 沿用这个任务原来的仓库和分支。

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京→上海 · 600 元以下</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="count">—</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">车次与票价</h2>
<div class="k-card">
  <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  <div class="k-empty" id="empty" hidden>还没有票价记录，每天早上会更新。</div>
</div>
<script>
(function () {
  var list = document.getElementById('list');
  var empty = document.getElementById('empty');
  var count = document.getElementById('count');
  var sub = document.getElementById('sub');

  function fmt(n) { return '¥' + n; }

  function render() {
    var trains = ducter.get('trains') || [];
    list.textContent = '';
    var under = trains.filter(function (t) { return t.price < 600; });
    if (!trains.length) {
      empty.hidden = false;
      count.textContent = '—';
      sub.textContent = '还没记';
      ducter.summary('还没记');
      return;
    }
    empty.hidden = true;
    trains.forEach(function (t) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = t.no;
      var s = document.createElement('div');
      s.className = 'k-row-sub';
      s.textContent = t.seats || '';
      main.appendChild(s);
      var v = document.createElement('div');
      v.className = 'k-row-value';
      v.textContent = fmt(t.price);
      if (t.price < 600) v.style.color = 'var(--d-good)';
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(v);
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    count.textContent = under.length + ' 班';
    var min = under.length ? Math.min.apply(null, under.map(function (t) { return t.price; })) : null;
    sub.textContent = under.length ? '最低 ' + fmt(min) + '，' + under.map(function (t) { return t.no; }).join('、') : '暂无 600 元以下的班次';
    ducter.summary(under.length + ' 班 600 元以下');
  }

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"trains":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":"二等座 · 余 12 张"},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":"二等座 · 余 4 张"},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":"二等座"}]}

--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] Done: the board is up with nights 2 and 3 filled in, and it'll update after each run.
  [an app card under the message: made "Nightly hawdb write benchmark", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 2246 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <div class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">Latest night</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="latest"><span class="k-none">No runs yet</span></div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">Writes per second and p99 latency</div>
  </div>
  <h2 class="k-h">Nights</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list">
      <div class="k-empty">Each night's result will list here.</div>
    </div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var latestEl = document.getElementById('latest');
  var subEl = document.getElementById('sub');
  var listEl = document.getElementById('list');

  function text(el, s) { el.textContent = s; }

  function render(data) {
    var runs = (data && Array.isArray(data.runs)) ? data.runs : [];
    listEl.textContent = '';
    if (!runs.length) {
      var empty = document.createElement('div');
      empty.className = 'k-empty';
      empty.textContent = "Each night's result will list here.";
      listEl.appendChild(empty);
      latestEl.textContent = '';
      var none = document.createElement('span');
      none.className = 'k-none';
      none.textContent = 'No runs yet';
      latestEl.appendChild(none);
      return;
    }
    var latest = runs[runs.length - 1];
    text(latestEl, latest.writes + ' · p99 ' + latest.p99);
    text(subEl, latest.night + ' · ' + latest.stalls);

    runs.slice().reverse().forEach(function (r) {
      var row = document.createElement('div');
      row.className = 'k-row';
      var main = document.createElement('div');
      main.className = 'k-row-main';
      main.textContent = r.night;
      var sub = document.createElement('div');
      sub.className = 'k-row-sub';
      sub.textContent = r.stalls;
      var val = document.createElement('div');
      val.className = 'k-row-value';
      val.textContent = r.writes + ' · p99 ' + r.p99;
      row.appendChild(main);
      row.appendChild(sub);
      row.appendChild(val);
      listEl.appendChild(row);
    });
  }

  var initial = ducter.get('runs');
  Promise.resolve(initial).then(function (v) {
    render(v || {});
  });

  ducter.onChange(function () {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get('runs')).then(function (v) {
      render(v || {});
    });
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"runs":[{"night":"Night 2","writes":"181k writes/s","p99":"4.0 ms","stalls":"0 compaction stalls"},{"night":"Night 3","writes":"182k writes/s","p99":"4.1 ms","stalls":"0 compaction stalls"}]}

<div class="k-sum">
  <div class="k-sum-label">北京出差 合计</div>
  <div class="k-sum-value" id="total">¥0</div>
  <div class="k-sum-sub" id="count">还没记</div>
</div>
<h2 class="k-h">花销</h2>
<div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
<details class="k-add"><summary>记一笔</summary>
  <div class="k-field"><label>日期</label><input class="k-input" id="day" placeholder="2026-11-23"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>what</label><input class="k-input" id="what" placeholder="午饭"></div>
  <div class="k-field"><label>金额 ¥</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>
  <button class="k-btn primary" id="save">记下</button>
</details>

<script>
function fmt(n){return "¥"+Number(n).toLocaleString("zh-CN");}
function draw(){
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  const list=document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML="";
  let total=0;
  for(const x of xs){
    total+=Number(x.yuan)||0;
    const row=document.createElement("div");row.className="k-row";
    const main=document.createElement("div");main.className="k-row-main";main.textContent=x.what;
    const sub=document.createElement("div");sub.className="k-row-sub";sub.textContent=x.day;
    const val=document.createElement("div");val.className="k-row-value";val.textContent=fmt(x.yuan);
    row.appendChild(main);row.appendChild(sub);row.appendChild(val);
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  if(!xs.length){const e=document.createElement("div");e.className="k-empty";e.textContent="这里会列出每一笔花销";list.appendChild(e);}
  document.getElementById("total").textContent=xs.length?fmt(total):"¥0";
  document.getElementById("count").textContent=xs.length?xs.length+" 笔":"还没记";
  ducter.summary(xs.length+" 笔,合计 "+fmt(total));
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick=function(){
  const what=document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan=parseFloat(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day=document.getElementById("day").value.trim()||new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
  if(!what||isNaN(yuan))return;
  const xs=ducter.get("expenses")||[];
  xs.push({what:what,yuan:yuan,day:day});
  ducter.set("expenses",xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value="";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value="";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);draw();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

    items = load();

### direct-stable-fact-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: Bun 是用什么语言写的？
  [partner message] Bun 主要用 **Zig** 写的，它的核心运行时和大部分底层代码都是 Zig。

另外还有两部分：
- **C/C++**：JavaScript 引擎用的是 WebKit 的 JavaScriptCore，它是 C++ 写的，Bun 通过绑定来调用它。
- **TypeScript/JavaScript**：一些内置模块是用 TS/JS 写的，编译后嵌进去。
```

完成标准：新用例通过；整个测试套件通过；把新用例的名字和运行结果报给我。

不在范围内：不合并、不推送、不开 PR。改动留在现在的分支上提交。
